'/storage.php';\n\nfunction respond(mixed $body, int $status = 200): never\n{\n    http_response_code($status);\n    header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');\n    echo json_encode($body, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);\n    exit;\n}\n\nfunction badRequest(string $message = 'Invalid request'): never\n{\n    respond(['error' => $message], 400);\n}\n\nfunction requestBody(): array\n{\n    $raw = file_get_contents('php://input');\n    if ($raw === false || trim($raw) === '') {\n        return [];\n    }\n    try {\n        $data = json_decode($raw, true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);\n        $GLOBALS['request_json_object'] = json_decode($raw, false, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);\n    } catch (JsonException) {\n        badRequest('Invalid JSON');\n    }\n\n    if (!is_array($data)) {\n        badRequest();\n    }\n    return $data;\n}\n\nfunction validRewardItems(mixed $items): bool\n{\n    $request = $GLOBALS['request_json_object'] ?? Other spellcasting classes can prepare spells, but do not yet\n    // have a slot progression exposed by this API.\n    return $class === 'wizard' && $level >= 1 ? WHERE campaign_id = ? AND character_id = ? AND item_id = ?');\n        $removeEmpty = $database->prepare('DELETE FROM play_campaign_character_inventory_items WHERE campaign_id = ? AND character_id = ? AND item_id = ? AND quantity = 0');\n        foreach ($ingredients as $itemId => $quantity) {\n            $consume->execute([$quantity, $campaignId, $characterId, $itemId]);\n            $removeEmpty->execute([$campaignId, $characterId, $itemId]);\n        }\n        $database->prepare('INSERT INTO play_campaign_character_inventory_items (campaign_id, character_id, item_id, quantity) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
